- **Why skipped — writing is the bottleneck, and a fresh note would consume a terminal slot.** Measured live:
  - 198 voids research notes on disk / 195 distinct subjects; 103 live voids articles; 103 archived.
  - Only **11 of 198** notes carry `consumed_by`, so the stamp is not a usable coverage signal. Coverage was determined by checking the slug against **both** `obsidian/voids/` and `archive/voids/`, never the stamp.
  - Unstamped but article exists: **144**. Unstamped with no article anywhere: **40 by exact slug match**.
- **CORRECTION TO THE 41-FIGURE: the true backlog is 33, not 41.** Exact-slug matching over-counts. Six banked notes are already written under an **alias slug** and are not backlog: `closure-types` → `closure-types-void` (live), `givenness-void` → `the-givenness-void` (archive), `infant-consciousness-void` → `infant-consciousness` (live), `silence-void` → `the-silence-void` (live), `surplus-void` → `the-surplus-void` (live), `the-death-void` → `death-void` (live). A seventh, `expertise-void`, is the subject of archived `expertise-and-its-occlusion` / `expertise-occlusion` (same Polanyi's-paradox content, confirmed by reading both). Net: **40 − 7 = 33 truly-unwritten banked subjects.** This is the `harvest-dedup-singular-plural-nearmiss` shape at section scale.
- **Five substring near-misses adjudicated by reading both texts, and all five are GENUINELY DISTINCT** — they stay in the backlog, they are not duplicates: `formalization-void` (Gödel/computability self-reference barrier to *proving* any cognitive limit permanent) vs live `interface-formalization-void` (the consciousness-physics interface resisting mathematisation); `modal-void` (confinement to the actual world; Lewis indexicality, List's quadrilemma) vs live `modality-void` (sensory-modality structure); `palette-void` (why qualia have *their* specific character; Levine's gap) vs live `palette-extension-void` (certifying an installed channel as new); `predictive-void` (cannot predict own future phenomenal states) vs live `predictive-construction-void` (cannot separate constructed from received).
- **THE STRUCTURAL FINDING — the pipeline is LIFO, so a fresh note does not merely add to the pile, it jumps it.** For the 64 voids articles with an exact-slug research note: **median research→article lag is 0 days**, 52 of 62 were written within 14 days, and **no article has ever been written from a note older than 69 days**. The four most recent runs all wrote within 0–2 days (`fusion-void` 2d, `palette-extension-void` 0d, `modality-void` 1d, `offloading-void` 1d). By contrast **18 of the 33 banked subjects are from February 2026 (~205 days old)** — the oldest 12, which is exactly the number of slots left, are all February: `contrast-dependence-void`, `intelligibility-void`, `persistence-void`, `individuation-void`, `modal-void`, `qualitative-novelty-void`, `participation-void`, `predictive-void`, `contingency-void`, `formalization-void`, `grounding-void`, `configuration-void`.

It argues the objection abstractly (`## The Objection Stated Neutrally` · `## Two Sources of the Missing Self` · `## The Map's Reply: A Locus, Not a Career` · `## Rival Readings at Full Strength`). This article is the worked ethical form at 8.101–102 and does not re-argue the abstract objection.
- **The spine, and the finding.** The sibling calls the residual dispute — whether "bad for the momentary locus" adds anything to "this feeling-moment is bad" — "close to terminological". The article's claim is that the verdict **survives metaphysically and fails normatively**: Śāntideva's inference *needs* the sufferings ownerless at exactly the momentary grain, so granting the momentary locus a bearer blocks the step from "no sufferings belong to anyone" to "they are to be warded off simply because they are suffering". A distinction can be idle in a metaphysics and decisive in an inference. Stated as a **correction to a verdict, not a defeat of the argument** — the tradition has the Parity Reply, and Harris's *upāya* reading holds the verses are not an argument at all.
- **Two-channel structure enforced**: §The Argument in the Text and §What Carries the Inference are source exposition only; §Where the Map's Reply Meets the Argument opens by declaring that nothing in it is attributed to the sources. Concept four-part format observed (neutral statement → the Map's reading marked as the Map's → rivals at strength → disputed implications split into "from the argument alone" and "only on the Map's reading").

## 2026-09-07T21:29:12+00:00 - refine-draft
- **Status**: Success
- **File**: [[topics/interventionist-and-counterfactual-dualism]]
- **Task**: `todo.md` L2134 (P2), chain successor to `research/interventionist-exclusion-and-chance-fixing-closure-2026-09-07.md`
- **Word count**: 2532 → 3686 (`ok` → `soft_warning`; **314 words to the 4000 hard threshold** — the budget is now thin for a hub article that accretes crosslinks). `scripts/curate.py` does not exist, so SKILL.md step 3 was skipped.
- **Changes**: new H2 `## The Equivocation Charge Against the Counterfactual Branch {#the-equivocation-charge}` (~940 words) inserted between "One Cause or Two" and "The Tenet-Diagnostic"; ~50-word forward reference added to the lead (named-anchor pattern); ~40 words added to the diagnostic's methodological paragraph; ~35 words added to the second caveat in "Relation to Site Perspective"; references 10–13 appended (no renumbering — the body carries no by-number citations, checked before inserting); `supervenience` and `psychophysical-laws` added to `concepts:`; one zero-cost piped wikilink to `concepts/functionalism` (path-qualified deliberately — the stem collides with `archive/arguments/functionalism.md`, so a bare slug would be stripped by sync).
- **Engagement with Rellihan: mixed.** Mode One for the exemption defence — the reply is earned inside Rellihan's own framework, using his explicit statement that the objection turns on the background-conditions conjunct and not on the laws, to show the wedge needs a functionalist role-realizer the Map does not posit. Mode Three for the residue — the second horn, and the open question of the psychophysical laws' form, are marked as genuine boundary disagreements rather than dressed as refutations. No mode labels appear in the article body.
- **THE PAPER-DISCRIMINATION FINDING — the task brief's own framing needed correcting.** The brief (and the research note) treat Rellihan (2021) as an attack on the *Noûs* paper this article cites. It is not. The **full text was retrieved** (Philosophy Documentation Center PDF, `pdcnet.org`, 8pp, `pdftotext`-extracted) and its reference list read: Rellihan's target is **Kroedel's Simple Argument**, canonically *Mental Causation: A Counterfactual Theory* (CUP) pp. 62–65, with earlier versions in Kroedel (2008) and **Kroedel, T. (2015), "A simple argument for downward causation", _Synthese_ 192(3): 841–858, `10.1007/s11229-014-0600-5`**. `Dualist Mental Causation` appears **nowhere** in Rellihan's references (grep-verified, with `zzz` negative control). So there are **two Kroedel 2015 papers**, one physicalist and one dualist, and "Kroedel 2015" is ambiguous — reference 11's annotation now says so. Kroedel's *Synthese* paper was also retrieved in full and its only `dualis*` hit is a reference-list pointer to the *Noûs* companion as "forthcoming".
- **What still connects the attack to this article**: Rellihan's abstract generalises ("consequences for **any** attempt to establish the reality of downward causation by appealing to counterfactual theories thereof"), and his **endnote 5** sets out Kroedel's dualist restructuring by name, defended "even when the mental supervenes on the physical with only nomological necessity". `concepts/supervenience` L83 says nomological psychophysical supervenience "is precisely the Map's position" — so the Map is squarely inside the dualist argument's scope. The article says so rather than escaping via Tenet 1.
- **The exemption defence, and its price.** Rellihan grants premise (1) "arguendo" because it "follows from a commitment to physicalism"; the deeper point is that his wedge needs a *conjunctive* realizer with a strippable **background-conditions** conjunct, and he is explicit the laws of nature are *not* that conjunct. Those background conditions are what let a neural state occupy a **functional role**. The Map's psychophysical laws attach experience to a physical state directly, so there is no separable background to remove and the (M_core & ~C & L) worlds the objection needs do not arise. **The exemption is bought by denying functionalism, not merely by denying physicalism.** Priced honestly: it is hostage to the *form* of the psychophysical laws, which the Map has not fixed — if what an experience tracks is a state's functional profile, the conjunct and the wedge both return.
- **What survives if the exemption is refused** (stated in the article, not just here): Kroedel's branch loses its claim to *establish* efficacy but, by Rellihan's own second horn, shows nothing about inefficacy; Mills/Lowe is less exposed (non-coincidence, not a realizer counterfactual); Tenet 2's selection channel is untouched (no realization relation, no counterfactual analysis of causation); and trumping is untouched **only because it never asserts the counterfactual under attack** — the same difference-making-freedom the article already flags as close to ensemble-level epiphenomenalism. That last point is fed back into the diagnostic as a *second methodological* reason for trumping, reinforcing the article's existing landing that the preference is tenet-underdetermined.
- **Second branch is not clear either**: Rellihan's endnote 11 engages **Vaassen, B. (2021), "Dualism and Exclusion", _Erkenntnis_ 86(3): 543–552, `10.1007/s10670-019-00118-1`** — a *different* paper from the *Synthese* exclusion analysis already at reference 3, and a second same-year ambiguity now annotated. Rellihan rejects its core/total co-causation move on future-similarity-weighting grounds.
- **Citation trap avoided (the task's central warning).** Nothing by **Vera Hoffmann-Kolss** ("Interventionism and Higher-level Causation", `10.1080/02698595.2014.915653`) or **Stern & Eva** ("Anti-reductionist Interventionism", `10.1086/714792`) was attributed to Rellihan; neither appears in the article. Crossref confirms Rellihan is sole author of `10.1002/tht3.502` (the second Crossref "author" record is the Philosophy Documentation Center, an organisation).
- **Quotation discipline**: fourteen quoted phrases, every one grep-verified against the raw `pdftotext` output of the two primary PDFs after whitespace/de-hyphenation normalisation, with two negative controls (`zzz-...` MISS; `Dualist Mental Causation` correctly MISS in Rellihan). Crossref records pulled fresh this run for `10.1002/tht3.502`, `10.1007/s11229-014-0600-5`, `10.1111/nous.12028`, `10.1017/9781108762717`, `10.1007/s10670-019-00118-1`, `10.1007/s11229-024-04500-7`, `10.1007/s11229-019-02481-6`.
- **Out of scope, untouched as instructed**: `concepts/causal-closure` (open P3 at todo L1819) and the deferred (1D) chance-fixing / Papineau–Sturgeon–Noordhof-via-Lowe work, which remains queued.
- **Published**: yes (synced to hugo)

- **Status**: Abandoned (reasoned decline — no merge, no file touched; the steady-state SUCCESS outcome per [[coalesce-candidate-pool-exhausted-by-role-granularity]])
- **Section pressure** (measured with `tools.evolution.state.count_section_files`, not the stale CLAUDE.md table): topics 328/360 = 91.1%, concepts 325/360 = 90.3%, voids 102/115 = 88.7%, positions 18/80. - **Pool movement since the 09:19 sweep this morning: ZERO articles added** (`git log --diff-filter=A` over the three sections returns nothing after 2026-09-06). The candidate-pool memory's own protocol says a same-method re-run on an unmoved pool is re-litigation, so no prior screen was repeated.
- **METHOD — ASYMMETRIC SUBSUMPTION, a tenth direction, orthogonal to all nine retired screens.** Cosine/Jaccard (screens 1, 9) are *symmetric*: they cannot separate "two overlapping peers" from "A wholly contains B". Verbatim shingles (screen 4) see only exact reuse. But the skill's qualifying bar is explicitly asymmetric — "one subsuming the other" — so it had never been measured directly. For every same-section ordered pair (S = smaller by prose words, L = larger), this run computed the **idf-weighted fraction of S's discriminative vocabulary already present in L**, plus S's unique-term residue. Standard recipe: frontmatter, Further Reading, References and house-style headings stripped, wikilinks reduced to labels, vocabulary restricted to terms in 2-8% of docs, terms required twice in a doc to count. N=755 docs, 3,489 discriminative terms, **104,779 pairs scored**. Distribution: max 0.615, p99 0.155, median 0.000.
- **WHY THIS DIRECTION MATTERED: it is the one screen that can defeat the dominant blocker.** Every prior run died on length — the 2026-09-06 pass found **0 of 7** groups feasible because summed word counts exceed the section CRITICAL ceiling. Subsumption structurally selects a *small* source, so the sums stay low: this run found **3 of 9 assessed pairs length-feasible under their section's critical ceiling**.   - **(5) MEDIUM — thermal grill corrected against Craig & Bushnell and DEMOTED.** Both defective sentences were false on PMID 8023144: "cold-sensitive **and pain-sensitive** spinothalamic channels" mis-rendered two classes that are both cold-responsive (innocuous vs noxious tuning), and "the delivered class is nociceptive while the stimulated channels are innocuous and thermal — a class error" inverted the unmasking model, which *releases* the nociceptive channel. Now: "their recordings came from two cold-responsive spinothalamic classes differing in innocuous versus noxious tuning, and the grill unmasks the noxious one. Genuine nociceptive traffic delivers the burn, so the channel tag is *correct* and the error is about tissue state — a veridicality failure rather than a mis-attribution, and a third exhibit this face declines." Retitled **"A near-case: the thermal grill"** and moved below McGurk so the two real exhibits lead. Verbatim quotation preserved. Two downstream consistency repairs followed: the deflations paragraph's grill instance was swapped for the McGurk capture, and §Direct methods dropped "the separated grill bars" from its list of mis-tag manipulations.
  - **(6) MEDIUM — ANSWERED rather than cut.** Decision recorded below. §Two Deflations → **§Three Deflations**; the irrelevance objection is stated in its strong form and answered in ~85 words by separating the faces: a neurobiological individuation makes the first-person answer *non-authoritative* on the count (conceded by the counting face) and leaves the attribution face standing, "where the question is not which carving is correct but why a subject is handed an unauditable verdict about their own experience — a stake no third-person taxonomy can retire." §Approaches/Indirect methods correspondingly cut back to "non-authoritative rather than merely unreliable" — the Keeley-at-second-hand support — with a pointer to the deflation. No inbound `#two-deflations` anchor existed (grep-verified across `obsidian/` and `hugo/content/`), so the heading rename breaks nothing.
  - **(7) LOW–MEDIUM — Tenet 3 mechanism corrected, near word-neutral.** Was "would select over contents whose channel it cannot read". Now "The tenet commits to selection among physically underdetermined *outcomes*, and the contents those outcomes go on to carry arrive tagged with a channel the selecting consciousness cannot read". Tenet 3 commits to outcome-selection and explicitly registers-but-declines the Process-1 relocation; "selecting over contents" sat nearer the declined option.
  - **(8) LOW–MEDIUM — the three absolutes replaced by an argument, not a hedge.** The principle is supplied in the lead and quoted from the register: "every such check is performed by the faculty whose causal status is in question" ([[positions/agency-and-will|P-A4]], on [[agency-void]]) — the same structure P-A4 already accepts for the agency void, which is what licenses (4)'s *occluded*. §Direct methods now argues the generalisation instead of asserting it ("each works by altering the *content* ... so what gets audited is the content rather than the classification that labelled it. For a content no manipulation has touched, the only verdict on offer is the one the classifying mechanism issued"). "Attending harder answers none of them" → "Attending harder settles none of the three".
- **Decision on (6): ANSWER, not cut.** The task's stated reason for preferring the cut — "answering it as a third deflation needs headroom the article does not have" — rested on the false ceiling. With 1002 words of real headroom the cut was the worse article: as written the clause was an over-concession against the Map's own first-person commitments and larger than Keeley (second-hand via O'Callaghan 2012) supports, and leaving it merely softened would have left the strongest objection to the counting face raised *after* the deflations section and never met. Answering it also pays a structural dividend the cut does not: the answer is what separates the counting face from the attribution face, which is the same separation (4)'s per-face classification needs.
- **Decision on the thermal grill: DEMOTED, kept in section.** It stays in §The Attribution Face but as an explicitly declined third exhibit rather than a supporting one. Removing it entirely would have lost the distinction it now earns its place by marking — veridicality error vs channel mis-attribution — which the void needs kept sharp, and would have left the Craig & Bushnell reference orphaned. Its demotion is also what makes (3)'s discount honest: the convergence is now booked as two demonstrations, not three.
- **PRESERVE-VERBATIM list confirmed untouched.** All 13 quoted strings in the file diffed identically against `HEAD` (extract-and-sort comparison); the only addition is the P-A4 self-quotation, verified verbatim against `obsidian/positions/agency-and-will.md`. The entire reference list is absent from the diff — **no reference edited, none inserted, nothing renumbered** (and the body was verified to carry zero inline numeric cross-references). The two secondary-source disclosures in refs 5 and 8, the two-qualification lead paragraph, the "Consonant, not probative — a physicalist reads the same signature as late convergence..." hedge, and **§Distinguishing Sibling Voids entire** are all unchanged. `apex/cross-modal-capability-division` unedited.
- **Attribution**: `ai_system` already `claude-opus-5`, the model composing this pass — left unchanged rather than plus-joined to itself. `ai_modified` from a live `date -u`. No `last_deep_review` created (was absent).
- **Validation**: `scripts/validate.py` ✓ Valid. Synced to `hugo/content/voids/modality-void.md`, both trees grepped.
- **Published**: yes

This is the over-concession pattern: a claim running *against* the Map, which reviews ratify rather than catch because conceding reads as discipline. **Fixed without upgrading** — the self-exposure is kept explicit ("the Map cannot press it as though it were exempt") and the debt is now *named* rather than left vague ("leaves the Map owing an account of its own individuator that it does not have"). ## 2026-09-07T10:41:07+00:00 - research-topic
- **Status**: Success
- **Topic**: The interventionist exclusion argument and chance-fixing formulations of causal closure (todo.md L39, P2; from outer review 2026-09-07 ChatGPT 5.6 Sol Pro, finding 1, article-level half)
- **Output**: [[research/interventionist-exclusion-and-chance-fixing-closure-2026-09-07]] (4153w)
- **Sources consulted**: 16 cited — **12 publisher-verified via Crossref DOI resolution**, 3 explicitly marked unverified, 1 second-hand
- **Task-note correction (the gap is narrower than commissioned)**: the brief asserted that no live article draws the interventionist conclusion, "verified, 0 files". **False.** `apex/born-preserving-causal-efficacy` already carries the do-operator formalism `P(O | do(C), X) ≠ q(O | X)`, the averaging identity `q(O | X) = Σ_C P(O | C, X) · P(C | X)`, a three-horn trilemma, and a "Natural distribution versus intervention" subsection added after a July 2026 outer review. `concepts/ensemble-level-epiphenomenalism` states the same dilemma in prose; `concepts/selection-only-channel` carries the decisive line ("marginal preservation is compatible with maximal conditional dependence"). Separately, Gebharter was **not** absent from the corpus — he is at lines 108 and 167 of `downward-causation-mental-causation-2026-01-15.md`, the "0 files" having been measured against live articles only.
- **Adjudication**: the reviewer's dilemma **as stated does not go through** — Horn 2 equivocates between the unconditioned marginal (which Born-preservation constrains) and the interventional conditional (which the interventionist criterion quantifies over). A **repaired** version — *intervention-invariant* chance-fixing closure — does bite, and the Map has already conceded it: its own apex text argues that preservation under intervention forces either horn (b) or a "psychophysical superselection" law, and names the Born-deviating route "the route the Map is likeliest to be pushed toward". So the true force is that the Map's answer **costs more than `concepts/causal-closure` admits**, not that it has none. Runs against Tenet 2; stated at full strength, not softened.
- **Genuine gap found**: chance-fixing closure has a named lineage the corpus does not cite — Papineau's completeness principle ("suffice by physical law to fix the chances of those effects"), Sturgeon's gloss, and Noordhof's (1D) "Every physical effect has its chance fully determined by physical events alone" — all quoted in **Lowe (2000)**, *Philosophy* 75(4): 571–585, `10.1017/s003181910000067x`, whose full text was retrieved and whose quotes were **grep-verified against the raw HTML** rather than summariser-reported. Lowe also states that physicalists favour probabilistic closure precisely to block the quantum objection, and dismisses Popper and Eccles as "an unrepresentative minority whom we can perhaps afford to ignore".
- **⚠️ Two AI-supplied attributions in the task's own subject line were wrong**, both caught by Crossref: "Rellihan, *Interventionism and Higher-level Causation*" is **Vera Hoffmann-Kolss** (`10.1080/02698595.2014.915653`), and "Rellihan, *Anti-reductionist Interventionism*" is **Stern & Eva** (`10.1086/714792`). Rellihan's real relevant papers are four others, of which **Rellihan (2021)**, `10.1002/tht3.502`, is a direct published attack on Kroedel's Simple Argument — a source `topics/interventionist-and-counterfactual-dualism` leans on and does not defend. Instance of `author-string-sweep-hits-two-real-papers-discriminate-by-title`.
- **Recommended chain target**: `refine-draft` on `concepts/causal-closure` (add the chance-fixing taxonomy and lineage; link forward to the apex rather than restating the formalism), **not** a new article — the argument is already written up in three places, and `topics/` has 1 slot left. A separate smaller task for Rellihan 2021 vs `interventionist-and-counterfactual-dualism` is flagged but **not minted** (research-topic writes notes only).

The scoping rationale is *not* the cap but the discovery that `concepts/teleosemantics.md` already carries a full 3,000-word steelman of the rival with the Map's own dissent, and `topics/the-naturalisation-failure-for-content` carries Hutto and Myin's master argument — a fresh literature survey here would have duplicated two articles one link away.
- **Engagement classification** (mixed):
  - Engagement with teleosemantics (Millikan, Dretske, Papineau, Neander): **Mode Three**. The in-framework refutation was attempted and does not land. The Map's two-normativities objection — proper function delivers only selection-relative correctness, whereas content requires answerability to evidence — is met by a principled bite-the-bullet reply that "content" simply names the historical kind, leaving a dispute about what content is rather than a result either side demonstrates. This is consistent with `concepts/teleosemantics.md`, which independently reaches the same verdict ("a reasoned bet about the limits of reduction, not a demonstration"); writing an in-framework refutation here would have contradicted the corpus's own more careful treatment.
  - Engagement with the active-inference wing (Friston, Clark, Hohwy, Seth): **Mode One**, partial and honestly scoped. - **⚠️ Three charges refuted, each by text the reviewer could have read.** (1) Its **Markov-blanket charge** — called "the most conceptually disastrous" and "disqualifying" — is answered by a section in `concepts/brain-interface-boundary` literally headed **"The Markov-Blanket Challenge"** (Pearl 1988 → Friston 2013 → Kirchhoff & Kiverstein 2019; Bruineberg et al. 2022 *The Emperor's New Markov Blankets*; Raja et al. 2021), plus `topics/predictive-processing-and-dualism` on **Beni (2021)** *Synthese* 199:6407-6427 and the disguised-property-dualism diagnostic. The audited article names that page **by name** at its REBUS section. (2) Its **alief / Comforting-Delusion charge** is answered at `topics/predictive-self-binding-and-the-naturalist-challenge`, which states Letheby's mechanism correctly and then presses Sjöstedt-Hughes (2022) against the naturalist baseline the CDO presupposes. (3) Its **"epistemic bankruptcy"** charge against empirical equivalence ignores that the article *withdraws its own discriminators* (L92, therapeutic durability) and states outright that the psychedelic evidence gives Tenet 3 **nothing** (L146). Fresh instance of [[gemini-outer-review-attacks-mechanisms-the-map-disclaims]] — in every case the answer was one link away.
- **⚠️ Its headline empirical charge is false as framed.** "The manuscript entirely omits the crucial, paradigm-shifting findings of Siegel et al. (2024)" — the article cites Siegel at L62 and L74 and as reference 18, metadata verified correct at Crossref (`10.1038/s41586-024-07624-5`, *Nature* 632:131-138, ~18 MRI visits/participant), and even reports the three-week anterior-hippocampus–DMN persistence. Split into the false framing and the one real sub-finding below.
- **Finding 1 (folded, highest value of the whole cycle) — Siegel's task-induced resynchronization is genuinely absent.** Grep returns **zero** for `task` and `grounding`. Verified at **PMC11291293 / PubMed 39020167**: *performing a perceptual task reduced psilocybin-driven FC changes*. A predictive account predicts this; a pharmacologically loosened static valve has no mechanism for an aperture that closes the moment the subject attends to a crosshair. **A filter-unfavourable datum from a paper already in the reference list** — the same calibration-asymmetry shape as the open metabolic-cost P2. Folded into the discriminator P1 as item **(N)**, scoped as one datum plus its hedge, explicitly **not** as "the article omits Siegel".
- **Finding 2 (folded) — the multidimensionality counterargument is genuinely untested, and the reviewer's citation is misattributed.** Grep returns zero for `bayne`, `seth`, `multidimension`. The objection is real: vividness and ego dissolution rise while metacognition, reality-testing and volitional agency sharply degrade, so the state may be a generative model breaking down rather than an aperture widening. ⚠️ The reviewer credits **"Seth and Bayne (2022)"** — a real paper (*Theories of consciousness*, `10.1038/s41583-022-00587-4`) that reviews theories, **not** dimensionality. The canonical psychedelic-specific source, verified at Crossref, is **Bayne & Carter (2018)**, *Dimensions of consciousness and the psychedelic state*, *Neurosci. Consc.* 2018(1):niy008 (`10.1093/nc/niy008`), with Fortier-Davy & Millière (2020) as commentary and Bayne, Hohwy & Owen (2016) for the framework. Corrected tuples written into the task; the reviewer's own barred. Folded as item **(O)**.
- **Finding 3 (folded) — the 2020s replication-and-blinding critique is absent entirely.** Zero hits for `replicat`, `retract`, `BDNF`, `blinding`, `van Elk`. Verified real: **van Elk & Fried (2023)** (published as "History repeating: *a roadmap*…" — the reviewer used the **preprint** title; an Erratum exists at `10.1177/20451253231223609`), **van Elk & Yaden (2022)** `10.1016/j.neubiorev.2022.104793`, and **Muthukumaraswamy, Forsyth & Lumley (2021)** on blinding and expectancy confounds. ⚠️ Scoped in the task as a **coverage gap, not a bad-citation defect** — the article cites no retracted work, and a fork must not hunt for a retracted reference that does not exist. The retraction alluded to is real and its metadata was recovered: **Shafiee et al. (2024)**, *J Psychopharmacol* `10.1177/02698811241234247`, **RETRACTED** (only DMT reached significance for peripheral BDNF). Folded into the citation P1 as item **(L)**.
- **Declined**: the broadband-fMRI / spectral-dynamics / neurovascular charge (weakness #2). The absence is real — zero hits for `BOLD`, `spectral`, `neurovascular`, `perfusion` — but the supporting citation is **misattributed**: it credits "Muthukumaraswamy et al. (2021)" with multitaper spectral analysis of resting-state fMRI showing a selective **0.01–0.06 Hz** power reduction, whereas the verified 2021 paper of that authorship concerns blinding confounds. The figure traces to **no** source and is barred from assertion anywhere. Also declined: the autobiographical-content charge (§6.2) — already answered at L146/L134/L136.
- **⚠️ Convergence accounting — this leg upgraded NOTHING, and that is the finding.** It contributed **zero** citation-metadata results. It never flagged reference 23 (the Timmermann chimera), so that defect stands at **2 of 3** reviewers, not three-way; and it never flagged reference 9 (Hameroff/Cambridge/2024, actually Gao ed., Oxford, 2022), which the Claude leg had passed as "plausible" — so that stands at **1 of 3**. Recorded explicitly so the synthesis pass does not read three same-subject reviews as three-way corroboration ([[convergence-can-be-false-two-reviewers-wrong-one-right]]).
- **No site-wide predictive-processing blind-spot claim this leg** — the four-times-recurrent false pattern needed no split, and no blocking gate or methodology change was proposed.
- **Loci**: all resolved to **live** articles (`topics/psychedelics-and-the-filter-model`, `concepts/brain-interface-boundary`, `topics/predictive-processing-and-dualism`, `topics/predictive-self-binding-and-the-naturalist-challenge`) — no archived-article-at-live-URL case.
- **Hostile-referee scorecard**: 6 weaknesses → 3 refuted, 1 refuted-as-framed (real narrow core), 2 real with corrected citations. **Two named citations attach real papers to the wrong content.** Consistent with the recorded pattern for this service on single-article audits.

## 21:16 - cross-review (deep-review skill, targeted mode)
- **Status**: Success
- **File**: [[voids/fusion-void]] — first independent lens on a page created 15 minutes earlier
- **Word count**: 1998 -> **1994** (`voids` 2000/3000 printed live; stayed `ok` and *gained* 4 words of headroom while fixing three defects)
- **Critical issues addressed**: 3
- **`last_deep_review` NOT stamped** and the archive is named `cross-review-*` not `deep-review-*` — a targeted cross-review is not a comprehensive pass, and either would have suppressed a genuine one via convergence damping.
- **Defect 1 — false corpus-wide superlative, 5 loci.** The page claimed its boundary "has been measured from outside, **which no other void in the Map's catalogue can say**". Refuted by [[voids/erasure-void]], which measures its own boundary from outside at population scale (Croy et al. 2012 congenital anosmia unawareness; Adams et al. 2017, 74.2% of a cohort with *measured* olfactory dysfunction did not recognise it) and draws the same kind of line (sudden post-viral loss *is* noticed — "the comparator survives the loss and does the announcing"). [[voids/mapping-mind-space]] is a second, weaker counterexample. The article's own taxonomy paragraph already said the calibrated thing ("unusual"), so this was an internal inconsistency too. Fixed at all five loci: fusion-void `description`, fusion-void lead, `voids/voids` catalogue entry (L196), `voids/voids` research-stage entry (L272), **and the upstream research note** [[research/voids-fusion-void-2026-09-02]] where the claim originated — corrected in place naming the counterexample so a future expand/replenish cannot re-mint it. Replacement keeps the genuine distinguishing feature: one experiment (Hillis et al. 2002) both establishes the limit and locates its edge — "a rare void whose own boundary is a measured quantity".
- **Defect 2 — claim attributed to the Map that the owning article disclaims.** "they belong on the brain side of any capability division, **and the Map says so plainly**". ## 19:26 - coalesce
- **Status**: Abandoned (reasoned decline — no merge; the steady-state SUCCESS outcome per the coalesce candidate-pool memory)
- **Cap state** (live, `tools.evolution.state.count_section_files`): topics 324/360 (90.0%), concepts 323/360 (89.7%), voids 99/115 (86.1%), positions 17/80. Unchanged since this morning's run; no cap pressure, so pressure argues *against* marginal merges.
- **Pool movement since the 08:56 run today: ZERO** (`git log --since=2026-09-04T08:56:29 --diff-filter=A` over the four content sections returns nothing). A same-method re-run would therefore be pure re-litigation, so this run searched from a fourth independent direction.
- **METHOD — LINK-GRAPH CO-CITATION, never previously run.** The three prior orders all measure the *pair itself*: TF-IDF content similarity (08-24 01:07), length feasibility (08-24 18:07), shared `coalesced_from` ancestry (closed 09-01), plus verbatim shingles (09-04 08:56). This run instead measures **how the rest of the corpus uses the pair** — build the wikilink graph over all of `obsidian/` (workflow and templates excluded), then for every co-cited pair score (a) **inbound Jaccard**, |in(A)∩in(B)| / |in(A)∪in(B)|, and (b) **inbound containment**, max(|A∩B|/|A|, |A∩B|/|B|), which finds a satellite the corpus never reaches independently of its parent. 788 articles indexed, **zero colliding slugs**; 78,057 co-cited pairs, 70,747 scored after requiring ≥4 inbound each and excluding apex (apex↔source overlap is true by construction — the calibration note from the 08:56 run, carried over and applied).
- **⚠️ THE RESULT INVERTS, AND THAT IS THE FINDING — co-citation measures FACTORISATION, not redundancy.** All five pairs read on the merits are heavily co-cited *because* a hub cites its deliberately-factored parts together, so a high score is evidence the corpus is drawing a distinction, not evidence it is duplicating one. This is the same inversion the verbatim screen reported this morning, reached from an independent direction — two orthogonal metrics now both score the Map's *health* rather than its redundancy.
- **Five pairs read on the merits, each declined on a role split the articles state in their own prose:**
  1. `topics/japanese-philosophy-of-mind-kyoto-school` 2461w + `topics/mesoamerican-nahua-philosophy-of-mind` 2093w = 4554 vs topics critical 6000 — **length-feasible, declined on role.** Both leads declare themselves data points for one argument: Kyoto is "a second [[chinese-philosophy-of-mind|basin edge]] for its [[cross-traditional-convergence-on-consciousness-irreducibility|convergence argument]]", Nahua "a *second* organicist test of its convergence argument … alongside the [[chinese-philosophy-of-mind|Chinese case]]". Identical to the indian/jain decline of 07-30: **the convergence argument's force depends on premise diversity**, and the two traditions share nothing but their role. DECLINE.
  2. `topics/frankfurt-cases-and-the-principle-of-alternate-possibilities` 2433w + `concepts/frankfurt-hierarchical-mesh-theory-of-the-will` 2259w = 4692 vs topics critical 6000, both `ok` status, highest inbound Jaccard of any feasible pair (0.429) — **the strongest candidate this screen produced, and the sharpest decline.** The concepts article's *first section* is `## Two Distinct Frankfurt Contributions`, opening: *"Harry Frankfurt made two landmark contributions to the free-will debate two years apart, and they are easy to conflate. **This article concerns only the second.**"* They cover two different papers (1969 leeway-attacking counterexamples; 1971 structural hierarchical theory) and one exists partly to prevent the conflation a merge would perform — the `where-the-substance-commitment-enters` pattern: **merging would re-inflate exactly what the article was written to eliminate.** DECLINE.

- **M1** Hoover's sign 63%/100% was uncited and bare. Verified **McWhirter, Stone, Sandercock & Whiteley (2011)**, *J Psychosom Res* 71(6):384-386, DOI 10.1016/j.jpsychores.2011.09.003 — unblinded suspected-stroke cohort, 337 patients, **8** functional cases, sensitivity 63% (95% CI **24-91**), specificity 100% (95% CI 97-100). Cite, cohort, blinding and both CIs added.
- **M2** The hypnosis half was uncited and its "substantial overlap" is not independent replication. Verified **Halligan, Athwal, Oakley & Frackowiak (2000)**, *The Lancet* 355(9208):986-987 — a **single case**, sharing two authors (Halligan, Frackowiak) and a paradigm with Marshall et al. (1997), and answered by critical Lancet correspondence (Terao & Collinson 356(9224):162-163). In an article built on convergence-without-shared-mechanism this is exactly the independence failure its own epistemology link warns about. Stated in the body; cite added with the correspondence noted.
- **M3** "unusually convergent … reproducible" presented one side of a mixed literature. The amygdala-SMA connectivity increase *does* have direct independent replication, so the claim was not fabricated — but amygdala *responses* have come out normal or hypoactive in some FMD and functional-seizure cohorts, most studies use healthy-control-only comparisons, and few adjust for near-ubiquitous mood/anxiety comorbidity. ## 16:55 - research-topic
- **Status**: Success
- **Topic**: Ontic structural realism and the Galilean exclusion
- **Output**: [[research/ontic-structural-realism-galilean-exclusion-2026-09-04]]
- **Task**: P2 — harvested 2026-09-04 from `reviews/optimistic-2026-09-03-explanatory-limit-wing.md`, which deferred the subject here rather than minting an article.
- **Sources consulted**: 18 cited; 10 web searches; 8 artefacts retrieved.
- **Headline finding — the rival does not exist in print.** The reviewer framed OSR as "the one live position that agrees with Galileo's exclusion and then denies there was ever anything excluded". Philosophically right, bibliographically wrong. **Raw-artefact keyword census** (curl + Python grep of the fetched HTML, not a summariser): SEP *Structural Realism* — written by Ladyman himself, rev. 2023-05-18, ~170,502 chars — contains **0** occurrences of "consciousness", "conscious" and "experience", and **1** of "qualia", that one being a citation of Unger (1979) for the view that *qualia are the non-structural components of reality*. Mirror-image: SEP *Russellian Monism* has **96** "consciousness" and **0** each of "French", "Esfeld", "relations without relata". The two literatures are effectively disjoint. The composite rival must be assembled from OSR-about-physics (Ladyman & Ross) plus structuralism-about-qualia (Loorits 2014; Fink/Kob/Lyre 2021; Lyre 2022) — and **both phenomenal structuralists decline the ontic reading**: Lyre, verbatim, "One might be inclined to ask whether neurophenomenal structuralism is a version of structural realism. The short answer is: no"; Loorits classes OSR as a "relatively small minority" radicalism he does not share. So an article would have to *build* its opponent, and must say so on the page.
- **Second finding — the Map already conceded this defeater twice, in one clause each.** `concepts/physical-completeness` says "The Map sides with the epistemic camp" (a declaration, no argument) while giving Maudlin — the opponent on the *other* flank — a full paragraph. `concepts/intrinsic-nature` lists structural realism dissolving quiddities as defeater **#1** and discharges it with "remains contested". Chalmers is more generous to that rival than the Map is, calling these views "respectable".
- **Third finding — it bites the route, not the thesis.** Eliminative OSR would establish that *physical* entities lack intrinsic natures, not that *experiences* do. It removes `physical-completeness`'s ontological-remainder argument; it does not touch Dualism. An article aiming it at Dualism will have mis-aimed.
- **⚠️ Correction to the record.** SEP *Russellian Monism* quotes Chalmers 2013 and appends "(e.g., Ladyman and Ross...)". Raw grep of the Amherst Lecture PDF (pdftotext of the publisher's own file): **zero** occurrences of "Ladyman" anywhere in it. The parenthesis is Alter and Pereboom's gloss, not Chalmers's citation, and must be attributed accordingly.
- **⚠️ Quote-fidelity catch.** Two summariser-supplied quotations were re-checked against raw artefacts. One failed for real: Lyre's "Every phenomenal character is exhaustively individuated by its relations…" is Lyre **quoting his own earlier co-authored paper** (Fink/Kob/Lyre, cited in-text as "FKL, p. 3") and immediately hedging — "We are sympathetic to this reading, but focus here on a methodological point". The summariser presented it as Lyre's flat assertion, attribution and hedge both stripped. The other was a false miss caused by my own case-sensitive grep (`if` vs `If`) and resolved to a genuine hit. Both are recorded in the note's gaps section.
- **Recommendation**: standalone `concepts/ontic-structural-realism` (or `topics/the-steelman-for-ontic-structural-realism`), scoped as the dialectic rather than a survey — with the fold alternative (two edits to `galilean-exclusion` and `physical-completeness`) written out as a genuine option rather than a strawman. - **The defect**: "If experience were merely information, memory could preserve it completely; that it cannot suggests experience involves the haecceity of *undergoing* rather than *representing*." The conditional is invalid — physical information is routinely compressed, partially encoded, corrupted, overwritten or lost, so "merely information" delivers no guarantee of complete preservation, and lossy memory is precisely what an informational account predicts. As written it handed the physicalist a free reply and rested a haecceity argument on a premise carrying no weight.
- **The transplant** (mirrors the two descendants; no third wording invented): the entailment is **retired, not softened**. An informational account promises nothing about preservation; what it owes is an explanation of *which* information goes missing and why the loss is total for phenomenal character while the propositional record of the same episode degrades only partially. The force is relocated to **indexical non-recurrence** — no stored fidelity delivers *numerical recurrence*, because yesterday's experience was undergone from an indexical position that does not come round twice. Vocabulary is now consistent across the chain: `voids/conceptual-metabolism-void` hands *numerical recurrence* to `inaccessible-past`, which hands it here and to the apex. **Argument 4 still does its work** — it remains the fourth of four considerations and still lands "the haecceity claim in temporal form"; no hole where the fallacy was.
